先週（11/2〜11/9）発表の主な経済指標
⽶国雇⽤は安定も、AI曝露度の⾼い職業で若年雇⽤が弱含
み。雇⽤不安が⺠主党躍進を後押しした可能性。AI関連株が
調整するなか、韓国中銀は投資減速のアジアへの波及を警戒
所定内給与は物価上昇率を上回る伸びが持続。冬季ボーナス
も押し上げ。電気・ガス料⾦補助再開は消費の追い⾵に。⼀⽅、
消費回復が進めば基調的な物価上昇圧⼒を強める可能性
注目点② 賃上げの波及と物価対策が日本の消費を下支え注目点① 米AI投資拡大の裏で広がる「不安」と「リスク」
⾮農業部⾨雇⽤者数は市場予想を上回り、失業
率も低下。ISM景況指数は製造業・⾮製造業とも
に拡⼤圏を維持。雇⽤・企業活動の底堅さの⼀⽅
で、消費者マインドは持ち直すも依然として低⽔準

（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
失業率
米国：雇用統計は良好な結果。一方で、賃金上昇もインフレ見合いで見劣り
 10月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋16.5万人と市場予想（同＋6.5万人）を大きく上回る結果
―過去2カ月も合わせて8.4万人の上方修正。業種別では専門ビジネスサービスやヘルスケアが全体をけん引
― 時間当たり賃金も前月比＋0.4％（前年比＋3.1％）と着実に増加。ただしインフレ率に割り負けている状況が継続
 失業率は4.1％と前月（4.2％）から小幅低下も、失業期間は長期化
― 失業期間の中央値は11.9週（9月：同11.5週）と長期化。27週以上の長期失業者も増加基調維持。企業の採用率は低下

傾向が続いており、労働者が失業状態から脱しにくい傾向が継続
3
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
非農業部門雇用者数
失業率は低位安定
雇用者数は市場予想を大きく上回る
過去2カ月もそれぞれ上方修正

（年）
米国：AI普及の影響は若年層に偏在。雇用機会への不安が拡大
 AI曝露度別の雇用指数をみると、22～34歳では曝露度の高い職業の雇用が相対的に弱含み
― AIの影響がキャリア形成段階の若年層に偏っている可能性
 若年層では、AIによる雇用代替に加え、物価高など生活面の不安も根強い状況
― AIを否定的に捉える割合も若い世代ほど高く、AI普及への警戒感がうかがえる状況
4
（注） 上図の若年層とは18～29歳。経済状況は回答者の主観的な評価

（出所） Harvard Youth Poll、ABC News、Washington Post、Ipsosより、みずほ総合研
究所調査部作成
AI曝露度別雇用指数
年齢別AIの認識：「AIは社会に悪い」と回答した割合
（注） 1．AI曝露度とは各職業の業務が生成AIの影響を受ける度合いを示す。職業を曝露度
に応じて5分位に分類し、低・中・高はそれぞれ第1・第3・第5分位を指す
2．年齢層別の雇用指数は、米国雇用統計の家計調査における年齢別雇用者数をウェ
イトに加重平均して算出。年齢区分が一致しない部分は、各階級内の雇用者数が均
等に分布すると仮定して按分
（出所）スタンフォード大学デジタル経済研究所、ADP Researchより、みずほ総合研究所調査
部作成
中堅・シニア層（35歳以上）
若年層（22～34歳）
若年層が感じる経済的脅威
若年層ほどAIへの反感が強い
若年層に偏る雇用への影響

（年/月）
米国：若年層の経済的不安が中間選挙の投票行動にも影響か
 2026年中間選挙では「物価高」や「雇用」への不安が若年層の主要な関心事項に
― こうした経済的不安が共和党政権への不満につながった可能性
― 年齢別の投票動向では、若年層を中心に民主党支持が優勢。生活費負担や将来の雇用機会への不安が投票行動に
影響した模様
― なお、議会でも世代交代が進展。若い世代の政策選好が今後の政策形成に与える影響にも注目

### Page 7

（参考）民主党が掲げる主な政策
 勝利した民主党は、若年層の不満とも重なる生活費負担の軽減（アフォーダビリティ）を共通の政策課題に位置付け
 具体的な政策手段を巡っては、中道派と左派・進歩派の間で違い
― 中道派は競争促進や規制緩和、財政規律を重視する一方、左派・進歩派は大企業への規制・課税強化や政府支出の
拡大を主張。今後の党内調整が焦点に
6

（年）

アジア：AI投資減速の影響が域内の貿易ネットワークを通じて波及するリスク
 韓国中銀はAI関連投資の拡大に伴う過剰投資やレバレッジの高まり、循環金融などの金融リスクを警戒
― AI産業の期待収益の過大評価や最終的なリスク所在把握が困難になり、リスクが過小評価される可能性があると指摘
― また、米国企業のAI投資が減速する悲観シナリオでは、韓国の半導体輸出が大幅に減少し、GDP成長率を0.4％Pt押し
下げると試算
― 韓国のAI関連財貿易は中国・台湾との取引が4割超。アジア域内で形成された貿易ネットワークを通じて、AI投資減速の
影響が広範に波及する可能性

日本：物価対策の再開が家計負担を軽減。消費の回復を後押し
9
 政府は冬場の電気・ガス料金補助の再開を決定。標準的な家庭で計7,000円程度の負担軽減を見込む
― 一方、ガソリン価格の補助は段階的に縮小（基準額を毎月5円ずつ引き上げ）。電気・ガス料金補助金の効果を一部減殺
 補助金を巡る政策変更は、コアCPIの前年比を最大0.4％程度押し下げ
― 賃金上昇に加え、政策による物価負担軽減が家計の実質購買力を下支え
― 物価対策がCPIを押し下げる一方、消費回復を通じて基調的な物価上昇圧力が強まる可能性には留意が必要

（注） ガソリンと同額の補助を実施している軽油・重油・灯油、ガソリン補助額の4割相当

欧州：10月消費者物価はエネルギー主導で加速。企業は販売価格の引き上げを計画
10
 10月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋4.0％と、エネルギー主導で前月（同＋3.8％）から加速
― 一方、コアは同＋2.5％と前月から不変。エネルギー高の二次的な影響はいまだ顕在化せず
 コスト上昇圧力が高まるなか、企業は販売価格の引き上げを計画。高止まりするインフレにECBは警戒姿勢を維持

中国：政策金利をリバースレポ翌日物に変更。銀行貸出金利の新たな決定メカニズムを模索
 中国人民銀行は政策金利をリバースレポ翌日物に変更（11/2）。短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙い
― リバースレポ7日物から切り替え、翌日物のオペを毎営業日実施。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）も翌日物金利を参照へ
― インターバンク市場における債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）が誘導目標と明言。短期金利の安定化を図る考え
 銀行貸出金利の新たな決定メカニズムを模索。DR001を参照して貸出金利を決定する方法を試験的に導入

― 背景には、銀行貸出の指標金利であるLPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、LPRより低利での貸出比率が上昇している実態
― 今後、財務力のある企業は銀行と協議の上でDR001かLPRを選択し、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを適用へ

― うち非製造業PMIが49.7と前月（50.2）から低下も、宿泊やスポーツ・娯楽が持ち直し、情報サービスは堅調だった

― 中東情勢に伴う価格上昇に一服感。原材料価格は56.8（同：60.8）、出荷価格は51.7（同：54.0）まで戻す

中国：PPIは生産財が急上昇も、川下への価格転嫁進まず。CPIは食品価格が下押し要因に

### Page 15

アジア：ASEAN5の製造業景況感は概ね堅調。原油高など仕入コスト上昇の影響は残存
 ASEAN5の10月の製造業景況感は、フィリピンを除き改善圏。受注・生産は好調も、コスト高が継続
― タイ、ベトナムは高水準で推移し、インドネシアは回復が鮮明。一方、フィリピンが基準となる50を下回る水準
― S&Pはリリース資料において、新規受注の増加が生産を支えたものの、仕入コスト上昇と原料調達・物流の制約を指摘
 ASEAN5では、生産者物価（PPI）が高止まり。原油高の影響が残存
― 原油高が続く中、石炭・石油に加えて化学やプラ・ゴム等の製品価格にも上昇圧力が継続
― 仕入コストの高止まりが、企業収益や生産活動の重荷となるリスクに留意が必要

 ASEAN5の一部の国で財政収支が悪化

― フィリピンは税収の伸び悩み、タイは景気下支えに向けた公共投資拡大や予算執行の加速などにより歳出が大きく増加

廣達(2382 TT) Trading Buy

AI 伺服器展望維持不變，投資建議 Trading Buy 
投資建議：廣達目前未見主要 CSP 客戶調整 AI 伺服器訂單，
且考慮到 CSP 對於記憶體、CPU 和 PCB 等關鍵零組件的鎖料
能力，我們預期 Vera Rubin 將於如原先預期於 1Q27 放量，2027
年營運展望維持正向，然而考慮到近期市場對於資料中心建置
的疑慮升溫而可能抑制評價，因此我們給予廣達 Trading Buy 的
投資建議，目標價 324 元。 
3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，稅後 EPS 6.92 元：3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，
QoQ+7.6%；毛利率 4.75%， QoQ-0.27ppts；營業利益 322.04 億
元，QoQ-6.0%；歸屬母公司淨利 284.22 億元，QoQ-0.8%；稅後
EPS 6.92 元。 
PC 市場需求疲弱，伺服器業務支撐營收季增： 3Q26 筆電出貨
960 萬台，QoQ-17%，考慮到筆電零組件漲價帶動 ASP 提高，
我們預估 PC 營收季減高個位數。通用型伺服器營收優於預期，
除 2H26 AI Agent 強勁的需求外，ASP 亦由於零組件漲價而提
升。AI 伺服器部分出貨量較 2Q26 微幅下滑，但同樣受零組件
漲價帶動 ASP 提升影響，預估 AI 伺服器營收仍季增低個位數。 
2027 年伺服器展望不變：近期市場對於限制資料中心相關法案
的擔憂升溫，然而廣達表示目前客戶方面並未調整訂單，就現
階段來看主要 CSP 的資本支出尚未放緩，我們預估 2027 年 AI
伺服器展望將維持不變。我們預期 1Q27 Vera Rubin 將可如先
前預期放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收高雙位數增長。考慮
到 AI Agent 的需求持續成長，預期 2027 年通用型伺服器全年
營收呈雙位數年增。

### Page 2

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 12 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG 
廣達積極推動環境、社會及公司治理（ESG）永續發展，並將節能減碳、人
才培育及供應鏈管理納入企業營運策略。環境方面，公司於 2024 年通過科
學基礎減碳目標倡議（SBTi）審查，承諾以 2022 年為基準，2030 年前將範
疇一及範疇二溫室氣體排放量降低 42%、範疇三降低 25%，並以 2050 年達
成淨零排放為長期目標。公司亦持續推動再生能源使用及能源管理，2024 年
綠電使用占總用電量 47.51%，並設定 2030 年再生能源使用比例達 50%的目
標。 
社會責任方面，廣達重視員工權益、職業安全及人才培育，遵循責任商業聯
盟（ RBA）行為準則，並透過廣達文教基金會及廣藝基金會推動教育與藝文
活動。公司治理方面，廣達持續強化風險管理、資訊安全及供應鏈永續管理，
並參照 TCFD 及 SASB 等國際框架揭露永續資訊，以提升營運透明度及長期
經營韌性。 
公司簡介 
廣達電腦（Quanta Computer Inc.， 2382 TT）成立於 1988 年，總部位於台灣
桃園，為全球主要電子產品原廠設計製造（ODM）廠商之一，主要業務涵蓋
筆記型電腦、伺服器、雲端運算設備及其他智慧電子產品。公司早期以筆記
型電腦代工為主要業務，憑藉產品研發、供應鏈管理及全球製造能力，建立
與國際品牌客戶的長期合作關係，近年則積極擴展雲端運算及人工智慧相
關業務。 
廣達透過旗下雲達科技（QCT）布局資料中心市場，提供伺服器、儲存設備、
網路交換器及整機櫃解決方案，並隨大型雲端服務供應商（CSP）擴 大 AI 基
礎建設投資，逐步深化 AI 伺服器業務布局。相較傳統筆電代工，AI 伺服器
對系統整合、散熱、電源管理及高速互連技術要求較高，帶動產品複雜度及
單機價值提升，成為公司營收成長的重要驅動因素。 
展望未來，廣達持續推進 AI 伺服器及機櫃級系統整合能力，並透過全球生
產據點布局，強化供應鏈韌性及在地化交付能力。隨 AI 運算需求持續擴張，
伺服器業務可望維持公司中長期成長動能，惟仍須關注 CSP 資本支出、產
品轉換時程及新平台量產效率對營運的影響。 
3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，稅後 EPS 7.08 元：3Q26 營收 1.12 兆元，QoQ+7.6%，
YoY+125.2%；毛利率 4.75%，QoQ-0.27ppts，YoY-2.10ppts；營業利益 322.04
億元，QoQ-6.0%，YoY+75.3%，營益率 2.89%，QoQ-0.41ppts，YoY-0.82ppts；
歸屬母公司淨利 284.22 億元，QoQ-0.8%， YoY+73.0%；稅後 EPS 7.08 元。
PC 市場需求疲弱，伺服器業務支撐營收季增： 3Q26 筆電出貨 960 萬台，
QoQ-17%， YoY-24%，出貨量略優於 2Q26 預期的 925 萬台，考慮到筆電零
組件漲價帶動 ASP 提高，我們預估 PC 營收季減高個位數。通用型伺服器營
收優於預期，主要原因除 2H26 AI Agent 強勁的需求外，亦由於 ASP 受零
組件漲價影響而提升。AI 伺服器部分出貨量較 2Q26 微幅下滑，但同樣受零
組件漲價帶動 ASP 提升影響，預估 AI 伺服器營收仍季增低個位數。 
零組件漲價及產品組合改變導致毛利率承壓：由於(1)筆電持續因成本上揚
而壓縮獲利，且出貨數下滑不利經濟規模(2)雖 AI 伺服器出貨量季減，但由

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 12 日

3 群益投顧 
ASP 提升驅動營收增長，造成毛利率稀釋(3)通用型伺服器出貨量小幅季增，
且 ASP 提升。綜上原因導致 3Q26 毛利率季減 0.27ppts，帶動營益率季減
0.41ppts，此一情景預估將延續至 2027 年，且我們預估 Vera Rubin 世代平
台 ASP 較 GB300 系列接近倍增，雖營收與獲利將可持續上升，但未來 margin
將持續承壓。雖目前有數個專案採用 Consignment model，然而主要以通用
型伺服器與 ASIC 平台為主，且出貨形式由 L6 至 L11 皆有，營收貢獻相對
於採用 B/S 的整櫃 GPU 伺服器而言佔比極低。據我們了解目前已有確定一
個 Vera Rubin 的大型專案及數個規模較小的專案將採用 Consignment 
model，但預估占整體伺服器營收比重仍低，對於毛利率的改善效果有限。
由於 B/S 將稀釋毛利率，Consignment 則會使營收下滑，因此未來針對 ODM
的獲利能力的評估重點應轉向以營業利益年複合成長率(CAGR)或是 ROIC
為主。 
終止華亞科技園區收購，2028 年產能擴充計畫維持不變：廣達表示，由於
對廠方規格及交付時程等事項無法取得共識，因此撤銷友達華亞科技園區
廠房的收購案，原先規劃的產能將透過海外投資計畫或其他彈性應變方案，
維持 2028 年投產的計畫不變。廣達 3Q26 資本支出 128 億元，2026 前三季
累計資本支出達 346 億元，預估 2027 年資本支出介於 400-450 億元，投資
金額成長幅度收斂，將主要用於改良租賃廠房及電力升級。管理層表示 AI
擴廠計畫為配合 CSP 客戶未來 12-24 個月的需求規劃，且伺服器屬於客製
化接單生產(Build-to-Order)，非承擔庫存風險的標準品倍或生產，資本支出
主要投入的項目為廠房電力設施及高階冷卻系統等通用的基礎設施，因此
縱然單一客戶或是特定世代的 AI 伺服器平台需求放緩，亦可無縫轉換產能，
較不會出現過去供過於求時，閒置產能導致的折舊與維護費用等沉沒成本，
營運風險相對較小。 
2027 年伺服器展望不變：美國期中選舉後，市場對於限制資料中心相關法
案的擔憂升溫，然而廣達表示目前客戶方面並未調整訂單。廣達的客戶以五
大 CSP 為主，Neocloud 的占比不高，就現階段來看主要 CSP 的資本支出尚
未放緩，我們預估 2027 年 AI 伺服器展望將維持不變。3Q26 AI 伺服器出貨
量的下滑主要原因為新舊平台交替的陣痛期，預估 4Q26 伺服器出貨量仍將
下滑，但因 Vera Rubin 相較 GB300 近乎倍增的 ASP，AI 伺服器營收將維持
季增。此外，目前在記憶體、PCB 及 CPU 缺貨問題對於消費性產品及通用
型伺服器影響較大，但由於廣達至 2027 年的訂單人以五大 CSP 為主，且
CSP 巨頭鎖料能力較強，因此 AI 伺服器的長短料問題相較之下較輕微，我
們預期 1Q27 Vera Rubin將可如先前預期放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收
高雙位數增長。通用型伺服器部分，受惠 AI Agent 的應用落地帶動通用型
伺服器需求增長，預估 4Q26 通用型伺服器將持續季增。考慮到 AI Agent 的
需求持續成長，預期 2027 年通用型伺服器除 Share gain 外，亦受惠整體市
場需求提升帶來的成長，全年營收呈雙位數年增。 
管理層預期 2027 年筆電市場持續低迷：管理層預期 2027 年筆電需求將持
續受料件漲價及缺料等問題壓抑，導致 2027 年筆電出貨量下滑高個位數至
低雙位數左右，考慮到目前筆電營收占比已不足 15%，因此我們認為 2027
年合併營收將受伺服器的強勁成長帶動而年增。 
預估 4Q26 營收 1.19 兆元，稅後 EPS 7.19 元：預期 4Q26 筆電市場仍舊低
迷，AI 伺服器處於世代交替的轉換期，出貨量將持續下滑，但受 ASP 提升
影響而帶動 AI 伺服器營收季增，此外通用型伺服器出貨亦維持強勁。綜上

4 群益投顧 
所述，群益預估 4Q26 營收 1.19 兆元，QoQ+7.0%：毛利率 4.62%， QoQ-
0.13ppts，毛利續持續下滑主要受到 AI 伺服器 ASP 提升帶來的稀釋，以及
筆電出貨量下滑造成的規模經濟效果衰減所影響；營業利益 331.68 億元，
QoQ+3.0%，營益率 2.78%；歸屬母公司淨利 288.35 億元，QoQ+1.45%；稅

預估 2027 年營收 5.99 兆元，稅後 EPS 30.85 元：群益預估 2027 年合併營

億元，YoY+21.5%，營益率 2.49%， YoY-0.5ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 1,266.99
億元，YoY+18.3%，稅後 EPS 30.85 元。 
投資建議：廣達目前未見主要 CSP 客戶調整 AI 伺服器訂單，且考慮到 CSP
對於記憶體、CPU 和 PCB 等關鍵零組件的鎖料能力，我們預期 Vera Rubin
將於如原先預期於 1Q27 放量，帶動 2027 年 AI 伺服器營收維持高雙位數成
長。通用型伺服器則受惠於 AI Agent 帶動運算需求及市占提升，預估全年
營收呈雙位數年增。預期伺服器強勁的成長將可抵銷筆電市場的低迷，帶動
2027 年營收年增，惟 Vera Rubin 的高單價將使毛利率及營益率承壓，但營
業利益亦將仍持續增長，整體營運展望維持正向，然而考慮到近期市場對於
資料中心建置的疑慮升溫而可能抑制評價，因此我們給予廣達 Trading Buy
的投資建議，目標價 324。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

研 究 報 告                
義隆(2458.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
深受 NB 出貨趨緩影響，短期營運受壓抑 
 投資評價與建議 
NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來源，
2027年營運展望保守，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但
延續性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，
且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：展望2027年，IDC預估
PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦
同處於逆風，主要動能來自非NB產品的無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身
演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品，1H26出貨
以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，
已通過雷虎測試並開始出貨，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，
也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，也是未來公司
重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而
言，短期NB客⼾拉貨恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較
受限。 
 投資風險 
NB需求進⼀步下滑或非NB貢獻不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況 
3Q26營運受NB影響而趨緩，獲利低於預期：3Q26營收22.2億元，QoQ-30.8%，主因1H26 NB客⼾提前拉貨及
缺料造成拉貨⼒道下滑，毛利率44.7%，主因產品組合轉差及供應鏈成本提升(包含模組的被動元件及PCB漲價)，
費用因嚴控成本而下滑至6.96億元，業外主要來自金融評價損失，EPS 0.58元，優於預期。 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾面臨CPU、記憶體缺料問題，拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但延續
性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估
毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：受記憶體與CPU缺貨持續壓抑NB出貨成⻑性，以及NB漲價、
AI排擠商用NB需求等因素，雖1H26提前拉貨推升公司營運仍佳，然自3Q26起NB客⼾需求趨緩，使得公司營運逐
季下滑，展望2027年，IDC預估PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦同
處於逆風，主要動能來自1)Haptic滲透率持續提升，因Haptic ASP達15美元，明顯優於傳統clickpad 2美元，將
帶動觸控板ASP提升，2026年Haptic營收貢獻約3.5~4億元(2025年約0.6億元)，2027年也將持續提升；2)ADAS
來自電動巴士，2026年出貨double，已有第六位客⼾開始小量出貨，2027年持續成⻑，主要來自政府電動巴士
補助所推升，政府最近也補助電動物流車，2027年確實落地後，公司將有電動物流車的成⻑機會，量也比電動巴
士多很多；3)無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP
產品，1H26出貨以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測試並
開始出貨，目前僅作前端光學模組，2026年底將推出避障功能，四鏡頭方案約500美元，十二鏡頭方案將達1,000
美元，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，
也是未來公司重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而言，短期NB客⼾拉貨
恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較受限。 
2027年營運動能受限，維持中立評等：NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來
源，2027年營運展望保守，維持中立評等，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins Reinforces AI Thermal Ramp; TP Maintained at 
NT$4,350
CITI'S TAKE 
AVC reported net profit of NT$11.6bn, topping Coto/cons estimates by 
2%/2% respectively, driven by margins beat partly offset by a non-ops FX 
loss. GPM improvement was above expectations on a richer liquid cooling 
mix, yield gains and disciplined raw material pass-through. Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC ramp-up and Vera Rubin ramp 
up. We model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM guidance also 
points to further sequential improvement on a higher liquid cooling mix and 
scale effects. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/27E capex of NT$15bn/15-20bn, 
alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, underscoring capacity 
conviction. We fine-tune our earnings forecasts to reflect updated guidance 
and maintain TP at NT$4,350 (25x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
3Q26 results beat on margins — AVC’s reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$11.6bn 
(+20% QoQ/+52% YoY) beat Citi/cons forecast by 2%/2%, driven by better-than-
expected margins, partly offset by a non-ops loss. 3Q26 GPM came in at 33.9% 
(+1.3ppts QoQ/+7.7 ppts YoY), above both Citi/cons estimates, mainly due to 
continued mix improvement, a higher liquid cooling contribution, yield gains, and 
disciplined pass-through of raw material price fluctuations to customers. Non-ops 
included a NT$304mn FX loss, which partially offset the strong core business.
ASIC ramp and capacity guidance underpin long-term outlook — Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC project ramp-up and early Vera Rubin 
generation contribution. We now model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM 
guidance also points to further sequential improvement vs. 3Q26, driven by a higher 
liquid cooling mix and scale effects. Mgmt. expects server compute tray liquid 
cooling penetration to reach 100% by end-2027. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/26E capex 
guidance of NT$15bn/15-20bn, alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, 
reinforcing confidence in capacity build-out.
Implications — We fine-tune our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts to reflect updated 
guidance and outlook. We maintain our TP at NT$4,350, still based on 25x 2027E EPS. 
We believe AVC’s solid supply position across key customers’ AI GPU and ASIC projects, 
combined with a sustained growth trajectory, continues to support its valuation. With 
visibility extending into 2027, we see limited near-term read-through risk from the 
ongoing platform transition. We reiterate our Buy rating on AVC.

Bull/Bear: Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

### Page 6

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

$17,183mn (OPM 14.2%)/consensus  of NT$17,047mn (OPM 14.4%), respectively. With
a narrower non-op gain of NT$94mn, net profit came in at NT$13,074mn (EPS NT

$23.4), 2%/5% short of MSe/consensus of NT$13,328mn (EPS NT$23.9)/NT
$13,748mn (EPS NT$24.5).
Outlook still on optimistic side: We expect Accton to grow revenue sequentially
into 4Q26. Network switch customers will likely continue to pull in more for their AI
clusters amid 800G migration. Meanwhile, its AI accelerator customer should ramp
more of its new generation product and increase adoption of liquid cooling thermal
solutions. To mitigate pressure from elevated component costs, it has negotiated

with customers for them to consign some components, e.g., DRAM, from late 3Q26.
Although this might cap revenue growth upside for Accton, it should help on the
GM side. We model revenue of NT$130,083mn (+8% QoQ/+81% YoY), gross margin
of 20.0% (+2.4ppt QoQ), operating margin of 15.5% (+2.1ppt QoQ)  and EPS of NT
$28.2 (+21% QoQ/+88% YoY). Looking to 2027, we see revenue growth of NT
$526,019mn (+26% YoY) and GM of 20.0%, for EPS NT$112.9 EPS (+31% YoY).

Stay OW: We believe Accton should continue to benefit from networking and
compute demand growth amid AI deployment, reflecting its system integration
capability, customer relationships, and execution. For the longer term,  it will likely
tap into opportunities in rack level solutions, optical-based switches, etc. We lower
our PT to NT$2,250 (20x 2027 P/E) from NT$3,500 (30x 2027 P/E), mostly owing
to more conservative sentiment toward AI names.
What’s Changed

Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW) From To

3Q26 GM Fell Short Slightly;
Better Margin Trend Ahead
Although 3Q26 earnings came up short on lower GM, we expect
Accton to deliver high single digit % revenue growth with GM
back to ~20% in 4Q26, reflecting some component consignment
arrangements.  Margin expansion in 4Q26 should be a positive
catalyst for the share price in the coming months.

Idea

Valuation Methodology and Risks
OW with PT of NT$2,250 (previously NT$3,500)
Price target (base case scenario value) –  NT$2,250 (-36%): Our new price target is based
on 20x our 2027 EPS estimate (30x previously). We lower our target multiple to factor in
recent market sentiment toward AI names. We see our target multiple supported by the
45% operating profit CAGR we project for 2025-28. This implies a PEG of 0.4 vs. its
average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for an operating

profit CAGR of 32%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,350 (-20%): We apply 30x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (36x previously). This scenario reflects stronger demand for network switches in
both data center and enterprise applications as well as more orders for AI accelerator
modules.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,800 (-39%): We apply 10x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (16x previously). This scenario reflects lukewarm data center and enterprise

capex and continued dominance by merchant GPUs in AI servers.
Downside risks to our positive thesis
• Weaker data center capex
• Slower network switch spec migration
• InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
• Softer custom AI accelerator growth
• Softer ODM direct business model adoptions

data trafﬁc (22% CAGR seen for 2023-30)

processing throughput.

customer base.

contribution from another hyperscaler.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 YoY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 YoY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지
면서 전년 동기 수익성 악화에 크게 영향을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조지아 텔루라이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스포티지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수요가 높은 북미 현지에서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중심의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추정치는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부정적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것으로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상반기 적용한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목표주가를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상대적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모멘텀에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 230: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0.pdf

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q 부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520 억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1 조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV 에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.

### Page 1

AI 芯⽚公司 Cerebras 宣布签署微软云合作协议，六个⽉内完成亚⻢逊、甲⻣⽂、微软三家
超⼤规模云服务商合作布局，管理层重申 2027 年营收三倍增⻓⽬标。制造端产能年底将提
升逾 10 倍，且因不使⽤ HBM 可有效规避持续⾄ 2028 年的内存短缺及涨价压⼒，形成供应链
层⾯的结构性优势。
摘要：
AI 芯⽚初创公司 Cerebras 在 2026 年第三季度交出⾸次实现净利润转正的答卷，并宣布微软成为
其六个⽉内拿下的第三家超⼤规模云服务商客⼾，公司重申 2027 年营收将实现三倍增⻓的强劲
指引。
华尔街⻅闻提及，当地时间 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇，备受市场瞩⽬的 Cerebras 公布 2026 年第三季度财报。公
司当季核⼼营收达到 2.309 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 70% ，⾼于分析师平均预期的 2.147 亿美元。
公司⾸次作为上市公司实现了⾮公认会计准则（ Non-GAAP ）下的净利润转正，达到 3000 万美
元。
随后电话会上， Cerebras 联合创始⼈兼 CEO Andrew Feldman 表⽰，公司同步上调第四季度及

全年指引，预计核⼼营收环⽐增速接近 40% 。公司还维持 2027 年营收 " 三倍增⻓ " 的⽬标，并预
期核⼼⽑利率持续向 60% 的⻓期⽬标靠拢。
Andrew Feldman 宣布，公司正式签署与微软的云合作协议，加上此前已公布的 AWS 和甲⻣⽂合
作， Cerebras 在过去六个⽉内完成三家超⼤规模云服务商的客件。他表⽰，微软协议的更多细
节将于明年上半年披露，相关收⼊亦预计于同期开始确认。
与此同时，⾸席财务官 Bob Komin 在电话会上披露，公司与 OpenAI 数据中⼼容量交付存在阶段
性滞后，已据此在剩余履约义务（ RPO ）中扣除相应延迟补偿额度。他同时强调，潜在取消量
占 OpenAI 合同总承诺容量不⾜ 5% ，公司预计于 2027 年下半年恢复正常交付进度。
制造端， Andrew Feldman 表⽰，公司正与 Flex 和 Sanmina 协同扩⼤产能，预计 2026 年底制造产
能将较年初提升逾 10 倍， 2027 年将进⼀步新增⼯⼚。
他还强调，由于公司不使⽤⾼带宽存储器（ HBM ），可有效规避持续⾄ 2028 年的内存短缺及涨

价压⼒，形成供应链层⾯的结构性优势。

⼤规模云服务商，还涵盖了主权云、企业客⼾和 AI 原⽣公司。
直⾯ OpenAI 合同争议： “ 延期是因为缺电和壳，不是缺芯⽚ ”
作为 Cerebras ⽬前最⼤的客⼾， OpenAI 的合同履⾏情况是投资者审视的焦点。截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，

管理层已将潜在的延期违约⾦从 RPO 中扣除。 Bob Komin 强调，该违约⾦对营收和⽑利率的影
响相对有限，原因在于违约⾦额占 RPO ⽐例较⼩，且将在未来数年内摊销确认。公司预计在
2027 年下半年能赶上进度。
Feldman 更是直⾔不讳地指出，瓶颈完全不在于硅⽚或系统制造：
他强调，不仅是 Cerebras ，包括 OpenAI 在内的所有⾏业巨头的数据中⼼建设 “ 都在全⾯延期 ” 。
同时， Feldman 坚决辟谣了关于 Cerebras 技术⽆法⽀持 OpenAI ⼤模型的传⾔：
打破 “ 低良率 ” 偏⻅，不依赖 HBM 成战略优势
今天，我们与微软签署了⼀项重要的云协议。
如果延期达到 90 天或以上，双⽅都有权取消该部分容量。但我们估计，今年可能⾯临取消的容
量将不到总承诺兆⽡数的 5% 。

我们有产能，我们有所有材料。我们等待的是⼟地、电⼒和外壳（ Land, Power, Shell ）的完
⼯。
有谣⾔称 Cerebras 因技术原因⽆法⽀持 Astra 。这些都是假的。我们的系统完全能够⽀持 Astra
规模甚⾄更⼤的模型。

⾯对华尔街对晶圆级计算（ Wafer-Scale Computing ）架构良率的质疑， Feldman ⽤了⼀个⽐喻
来进⾏反击：

WSE-5 芯⽚已回⽚，明年夏天量产
在技术迭代上， Cerebras 继续保持激进的步伐。
公司宣布，其下⼀代处理器 Wafer-Scale Engine 5 （ WSE-5 ）已经从代⼯⼚回⽚，进度超前。
Feldman ⾃豪地表⽰：
基于台积电 5 纳⽶⼯艺的 CS-5 系统将在 2027 年初夏投⼊⽣产，性能将⽐ CS-4 翻倍。
此外，公司还率先推出了异构解耦推理（ DISAG ）⽅案，将 AMD 的 GPU ⽤于预填充（ Pre-fill ），
将 Cerebras 的 CS-3 系统⽤于解码，⼤幅提⾼了每兆⽡的算⼒产出和利润率。
针对市场上关于低批次 GPU 可挑战 Cerebras 推理速度的讨论， Andrew Feldman 在问答环节表
⽰， GPU 通过降低批次规模可换取⼀定速度提升，但代价是单位 Token 成本⼤幅上升。
他指出，在每秒约 250 ⾄ 300 个 Token 的速度区间以上， Cerebras 的经济性已明显占优；在每秒
800 ⾄ 2000 个 Token 区间， GPU 在技术上已⽆法达到同等性能。

Cerebras Q3 财报电话会全⽂（ AI 辅助翻译）：
Cerebras 发明了⼀种全新的计算架构。⽤为其他架构设计的指标和思维模型来套⽤我们，就像
检查⼀辆特斯拉，然后问：排⽓管在哪⾥？电动汽⻋需要不同的参照系，晶圆级计算也是如此。
我们认为，内存短缺及其相关的价格上涨将持续到 2028 年，这将推⾼竞争对⼿的成本并限制其
供应。全⾏业的内存挑战，对 Cerebras 来说正是机会。
WSE-5 于 6 ⽉ 15 ⽇流⽚。芯⽚现已返回，已点亮，并已经在运⾏模型，正在进⾏机箱内测试。在
不到五个⽉的时间内从流⽚进⼊系统测试，这是⾮凡的执⾏⼒。

展望未来数⽉，我们看到两个确定性趋势：快速变化与算⼒短缺。过去 90 天⾥， AI ⽣态系统⻅
证了前沿模型领导者数度易主，数⼗款有价值的开源模型相继发布，重⼤安全事件相继发⽣，强
⼤的新型消费者智能体不断涌现，还有许多其他发展动态。
任何⼈都⽆法预测 90 天后 AI 的能⼒⾛向，更遑论⼀年之后。然⽽，在所有这些变化中，智能与
速度始终是每⼀个应⽤场景的两⼤核⼼要素 —— ⽆论是编程、安全，还是个⼈智能体。

在这个快速变化的世界中， Cerebras 的使命是确保⽆论 AI 如何发展， Cerebras 都能持续增⻓。
基础模型增⻓，我们增⻓；开源模型增⻓，我们增⻓；个⼈智能体增⻓，我们增⻓；⽆论是企业
还是初创公司增⻓，我们增⻓；⽆论客⼾需要硬件还是云计算资源，我们都能增⻓。我们的⽬标
是，⽆论什么因素驱动⾏业发展， Cerebras 都能赢得先机。
第三季度，我们在这⼀⽬标上取得了显著进展。
⾸先，我们超越了所有业绩指引⽬标。在对这些成绩感到满意的同时，我认为更重要的是 —— ⾃
上次财报电话会议以来，我们与多家 AI ⾏业领军企业达成了重要合作，最新进展是今天宣布与微
软签署了⼀项重要云服务协议。加上此前宣布的 AWS 和甲⻣⽂合作，迄今已有三家超⼤规模云
服务商选择将 Cerebras 平台纳⼊其基础架构战略。
这些新合作，叠加现有客⼾的持续增⻓，使我们得以全⾯上调第四季度核⼼业绩指引，并继续预
期 2027 年营收实现三倍增⻓，同时⽑利率持续扩张。 Bob 将在其发⾔部分详细介绍第三季度表

现及前瞻性指引。
关于业务结构

### Page 5

作为⼀家 AI 加速器公司，我们的核⼼创新在于芯⽚、系统和软件，使 AI 计算更快速。我们是⼀家
AI 硬件公司，拥有两条市场渠道。
第⼀条渠道是系统销售：将 Cerebras 硬件机架安装在客⼾的数据中⼼。这是⼀项优质业务，收
⼊确认快、回款快，但也具有⼀定的不规律性，取决于客⼾数据中⼼是否具备接收硬件的能⼒。
第⼆条渠道是我们的云服务。 AI 原⽣企业这⼀快速增⻓的客群，更倾向于消费算⼒（ token ），⽽
⾮⾃⾏部署硬件。鉴于数据中⼼和算⼒资源极为稀缺，我们同时拥有系统和数据中⼼，构成了重
要的战略优势。
对于⾃主⽣产硬件的公司⽽⾔， AI 云业务尤为出⾊。我们每兆⽡的成本远低于新兴云服务商，因
此能够实现更⾼的资本效率。两条渠道的业务⽐例会因季度⽽异，但我们相信两者都将随时间⼤
幅增⻓。
关于与 OpenAI 合作的市场猜测
OpenAI 是我们的重要⼤客⼾，也是紧密合作伙伴，我相信未来双⽅将继续保持这⼀关系。
OpenAI 在第三季度相较第⼆季度实现了增⻓，我们预计未来还将进⼀步增⻓。

OpenAI 基于 Cerebras 推出的 UltraFast SOL5-6 预览版取得了压倒性的成功，有⼒证明了市场对
快速 token 的需求极为巨⼤。
与此同时， Cerebras 确实没有⾜够的算⼒⽀持 UltraFast 在包括 Apsara 在内的更⼤范围内推出。
正如业界所述，我们认为算⼒是⾏业商业决策的关键驱动因素。事实上，即使 OpenAI 拥有海量
GPU 供应，仍需实施基于使⽤量的限制措施。
协议签署后，我们花费的时间⽐预期更⻓，才找到合同并上线满⾜协议要求所需的⼤量数据中⼼
增量产能。因此，我们⽬前在初始阶段的执⾏上有所滞后。
鉴于这⼀合作对我们业务的重要性，我们理解投资者对其给予⾼度关注。关于协议细节或相关新
闻，信息来源不太可能来⾃ Cerebras 或 OpenAI 以外的渠道。
例如，此前有传⾔称 Cerebras 出于技术原因⽆法⽀持 Astra ，这是错误的。 CS3 、 CS4 和 CS5 完
全有能⼒⽀持 Astra 级别的模型，乃⾄更⼤规模的模型。

同时，我预期并建议投资者预期， OpenAI 将利⽤多种合作关系和多样化硬件⽅案构建算⼒。
OpenAI 将淘汰旧模型，在这些计算资源上部署新模型，⽤于内部和外部⽤途。他们的算⼒需求
极为庞⼤，不同解决⽅案在其规模化进程中均有机会发挥作⽤。
关于推理速度的竞争地位
在此我想补充⼀点：尽管市场上存在各种实验性⽅案 —— 低批次 GPU 、初创公司发布、超⼤型
计算核⼼、超⼤规模云服务商的内部定制芯⽚ —— 我们依然是毫⽆争议的最快推理解决⽅案。我
们相信，我们的产品路线图将使我们在未来数年内保持这⼀优势。
业务评估框架：客⼾、算⼒、能⼒
我们对业务进展的评估框架保持不变，围绕三个维度进⾏：客⼾、算⼒和能⼒。
客⼾
我们正在快速拓展客⼾群，以扩⼤覆盖范围、多元化收⼊来源。第三季度是成果丰硕的⼀个季
度。我们赢得了两家新超⼤规模云服务商的合作。

### Page 6

我们与甲⻣⽂签署协议，将 Cerebras 硬件引⼊甲⻣⽂云， Cerebras 将部署在甲⻣⽂数据中⼼，
服务于 AI 实验室、企业客⼾以及构建交互式智能应⽤的开发者。我们预计由 Cerebras 驱动的甲
⻣⽂云推理服务将于 2027 年夏季正式上线。
我们还与微软签署了令⼈振奋的合作协议，相关详情将于明年进⼀步披露。
⾄此，我们在过去六个⽉内已赢得三家超⼤规模云服务商的合作，这是重要的⾥程碑成就，为我
们 2027 年和 2028 年的发展奠定了良好基础。
我们还与多家⾼质量新兴云服务商扩⼤了合作，包括 Firework 等，进⼀步多元化我们的市场渠
道，为客⼾提供更多访问 Cerebras ⾼速推理服务的途径。
开源权重模型同样为我们带来了助⼒，这些模型持续催⽣新应⽤，拓展 AI 在⽇常⽣活和各⾏业中
的应⽤边界。例如，就在本周，我们宣布 Cerebras 快速推理服务正在为 Built 公司的 OS 平台及个
⼈助⼿提供⽀持，为数百万⽤⼾带来极速推理体验。
通过 Built 这样的消费者应⽤，我们将 Cerebras 的强⼤算⼒直接送到⽤⼾⼿中。这正是快速 token

能够获得溢价的原因 —— 我们的云客⼾告诉我们，快速 token 能产⽣出⾊的⽑利率，⽽ Cerebras
的快速 token 是他们销售的利润最⾼的 token 。
算⼒
算⼒包含两个组成部分：数据中⼼算⼒和制造产能，两者对于业务增⻓都⾄关重要。
数据中⼼可⽤性是整个⾏业⾯临的持续性制约，我们也不例外。此外，我们认为美国政策环境的
演变可能会为美国境内的数据中⼼建设带来更⼤挑战。
2027 年，我们超过 75% 的数据中⼼产能将在美国境外上线。实际上，未来⼏个⽉内，加拿⼤、
法国和挪威的新设施将陆续投⼊运营。本季度，我们还签署了挪威和芬兰新增产能的合同，进⼀
步扩⼤了欧洲业务布局。同时，我们正处于在圣保罗签约数据中⼼的最后阶段，这将是我们在南
美洲的⾸个数据中⼼。
迄今为⽌，我们已为 2027 年底前交付签署了约 700 兆⽡的数据中⼼产能合同。我们的⾃有云已跻
⾝⾮超⼤规模云服务商中规模较⼤之列，⽬前正深⼊推进吉⽡级产能的谈判。
提升数据中⼼产能的最佳途径，是提升数据中⼼硬件的 token ⽣成能⼒，即每兆⽡产⽣更多

token。为此，分解式推理（ disaggregated inference ）通过将数据中⼼的 token 交付能⼒提升
最多 5 倍，有效扩⼤了我们的产能。每兆⽡产⽣更多超快 token ，意味着每个数据中⼼创造的收
⼊将显著增加。
在制造和供应链⽅⾯，我们正与 Flex 和 Sanmina 协⼒扩⼤制造规模，预计 2026 年底的制造产能
将超过年初的 10 倍。 2027 年，我们计划增设⼯⼚、进⼀步提升产能，以⽀撑预期的⼤幅增⻓。
我们的供应链也已做好⽀持这⼀增⻓的充分准备。
最重要的是，我们已获得台积电充⾜的晶圆产能承诺，台积电持续彰显其世界级合作伙伴的实
⼒。
同样值得关注的是我们供应链所不需要的东西 —— 这对我们同样⾄关重要。与其他竞争对⼿不
同，我们不使⽤ HBM 内存。我们认为，内存短缺及由此引发的价格上涨将持续⾄ 2028 年，这将
推⾼竞争对⼿的成本并限制其供应。⾏业性内存挑战为 Cerebras 创造了机遇。
⼯⼚和供应是扩⼤产能的必要条件，但并不充分，我们还必须保持⾼良率。

### Page 7

关于良率问题
Cerebras 发明了⼀种全新的计算架构。⽤针对其他架构设计的指标和思维模型来衡量我们，就
如同检查⼀辆特斯拉时追问排⽓管在哪⾥。晶圆级计算需要不同的参照框架，正如电动汽⻋需要
不同的评估维度⼀样。
我们欢迎就设计的各个⽅⾯提出问题，并将努⼒更清晰地分享架构细节。
然⽽，⾦融界存在⼀些持续流传的不实之词：⼀是声称我们的系统⽆法⽀持⼤型模型 —— 这显然
是错误的，因为我们⽬前已在运⾏⼤型模型；⼆是声称我们的制造良率低。两者均不属实。
关于模型规模问题，我已在前⾯的发⾔及此前的讨论中做出了回应。由于制造良率较难从外部观
察，我将在此直接加以说明。
良率分为两类：晶圆⼚良率和参数良率。我们来⾃台积电的晶圆⼚良率很⾼，参数良率同样如
此。我们的技术博客对此有详细介绍，以下做简要说明。
所有晶圆都含有缺陷。⼏⼗年来，这些缺陷是业界认为晶圆级处理器不可能实现的核⼼原因。我
们通过冗余设计解决了这⼀问题。
我们的设计包含约⼀百万个完全相同的计算单元（ tile ），其中⼀⼩部分作为备⽤。当缺陷导致某

个单元失效时，硬件会⾃动绕过它并激活备⽤单元。这⼀机制在晶圆⼚⽣产阶段和现场使⽤阶段
均有效，实现了⾼晶圆良率和强⼤的现场可靠性。
第⼆个挑战是参数变异（ parametric variation ）。即使硅功能正常，其电⽓特性也会在晶圆各处
存在差异 —— 在相同电压下，某些区域运⾏速度更快或更慢。这是芯⽚设计的基本现实，也是实
现晶圆级计算必须克服的障碍之⼀。
我们通过三种⽅式解决参数变异问题：
第⼀，  去除晶圆外缘。外缘是晶圆最薄弱的部分，也是参数变异的主要来源。我们使⽤适合 300
毫⽶晶圆的最⼤正⽅形区域，剔除⼤量边缘硅⽚。
第⼆，  针对宽泛的⼯况进⾏设计和测试。我们的核⼼能够容忍更⼤范围的变异。在时序收敛
（即验证电路满⾜时序要求的过程）阶段，我们在远⽐⾏业常规更宽泛的⼯作条件下对设计进⾏
验证，从⽽确保在晶圆各处变异的情况下仍能实现预期性能。
第三，  在晶圆各区域独⽴调节电压。我们发明了⼀种技术，可在晶圆的 84 个分区内独⽴调节核
⼼电压 —— 对较慢区域提⾼电压，对较快区域降低电压，从⽽对性能进⾏精确控制。据我们所

知，⽬前没有其他芯⽚具备这⼀能⼒。
最终结果是在晶圆⼚和⼯⼚两个环节均实现了极⾼的良率。我们通过⼯程化⼿段解决了其他⼈认
为使晶圆级计算不可能实现的问题，从⽽达成了这⼀成就。
最后补充⼀点：有⼈将我们硬件出货的相对较低⽑利率视为良率问题的证据，⽽实际上这仅仅反
映了我们制造能⼒的持续提升过程，以及在早期客⼾特殊定价协议下的最终批次出货情况。我们
预计，未来⼏个季度硬件⽑利率将⼤幅改善，最终超越 50% 的⻓期⽬标。
能⼒
我们在各个⽅⾯持续提升能⼒：新系统与处理器、改进的软件、先进的制造技术，以及提供⾏业
最佳解决⽅案的合作伙伴关系。

### Page 8

尽管 AI ⾏业变化节奏极快，推理速度的重要性仍在持续提升， Cerebras 依然是⽆可争议的领导
者。我们相信这⼀性能优势将⻓期持续，为此我们的产品路线图持续快速推进。
CS4 即将进⼊全⾯量产，性能和吞吐量均将翻倍。
我们的下⼀代处理器 —— 晶圆级引擎 5 （ WSE-5 ），已部署于第五代 CS5 系统，已从晶圆⼚如期
归来，甚⾄早于计划。 WSE-5 于 6 ⽉ 15 ⽇完成流⽚，芯⽚已回⼚启动，正在运⾏模型，并已进⼊
整机测试阶段。从流⽚到系统测试不⾜五个⽉，是出⾊的执⾏⼒体现。 CS5 的速度将⽐ CS4 提升
⼀倍以上，预计于 2027 年初夏进⼊量产。 CS5 基于台积电 5 纳⽶⼯艺节点，该节点的晶圆竞争相
对不那么激烈，晶圆成本也⼤幅降低。
我们的创新还延伸⾄完整解决⽅案领域。据我们所知， Cerebras 是⾸家实现⽣产级异构分解式
推理的公司。⽬前，我们的云服务正在使⽤ AMD GPU 处理预填充（ pre-fill ），使⽤ Cerebras
CS3 系统处理解码（ decode ）。为⽀持⽣产级上线，我们构建了⼀套分解式推理软件平台，兼容

AMD 及其他 GPU 产品，并将在 Cerebras 推理云及部分数据中⼼客⼾中部署同款⽅案。
分解式推理在相同功耗下能够提供更多⾼速 token ，这意味着云服务商每兆⽡获得更⾼利润， AI
⽤⼾获得更低的每 token 成本。我们相信分解式推理将成为主流云部署的默认配置之⼀。
我们的软件栈持续成熟，云服务实现了令⼈印象深刻的⾼可⽤性，系统运⾏速度不断提升，新模
型的启动时间也已缩短⾄仅需⼏天。
Cerebras 是 Kimi K3.1 的零⽇上线合作伙伴 ——Kimi K3.1 是⽉之暗⾯旗下的 2.8 万亿参数前沿级
开源权重模型，或许是⽬前市场上最顶尖的开源权重模型。 Cerebras 上运⾏的 Kimi K3.1 不仅是
业界最快，运⾏速度⾼达每秒 2000 个 token 。
⾛过近 11 年的历程，我们继续以惊⼈的速度创新。我们的团队在顺境与逆境中始终凝聚在⼀
起，公司内部的活⼒与激情从未如此⾼涨。
我们为解决⾏业中最艰难的问题⽽感到⾃豪。
总结：  我们超越所有指标，全⾯上调第四季度指引，并预期 2027 年营收三倍增⻓。我们在各核

⼼领域取得重要进展，包括新增两家超⼤规模云服务商客⼾，在过去六个⽉内已累计赢得三家超
⼤规模云服务商。 CS4 已开始出货，第五代处理器 WSE-5 已回⼚，并以创纪录的速度完成 CS5
整机测试。 AMD ⽣产级分解式推理已上线，对另⼀款 2.8 万亿参数模型的零⽇⽀持也以⾏业最佳
性能成功实现。
下⾯，我将发⾔权移交给 Bob 。
Bob Komin （⾼级副总裁兼⾸席财务官）：谢谢 Andrew ，⼤家下午好。在介绍第三季度业绩和
第四季度展望的具体内容之前，我想先回顾⼀下我们的业务规模化历程。
业务战略背景
我们选择在 2025 年初启动云服务，原因在于在这个快速演进的 AI 格局中， AI 原⽣企业希望快速
扩展算⼒，⽽不愿⾃建数据中⼼。由于超⼤规模云服务商会提前数年规划其数据中⼼算⼒分配，
往往⽆法为新硬件提供近期的⾃有设施空间。为了赢得基础模型实验室和超⼤规模云服务商所需
的⼤型部署合同， Cerebras 需要证明我们的系统能够在规模化场景下提供⾏业领先的推理性
能。在私有云中充分评估我们的解决⽅案之后，这些客⼾可以选择购买硬件，或采购我们的云服

务。
为了尽快提供⾜够的云算⼒以满⾜这些需求，我们选择尽快租⽤数据中⼼产能，将私有云规模向
吉⽡级推进。这⼀总体战略被证明是正确的 —— 数据中⼼产能⽬前已极为稀缺，⽽我们已有⼤量

### Page 9

产能在合同中锁定。
更重要的是，拥有私有云之后，我们得以向整个市场展⽰快速推理的价值，并进⼀步激发市场需
求。由于新型⼤型数据中⼼从⽴项到投⼊使⽤需要 15 ⾄ 18 个⽉，为了加快满⾜我们已有的⼤量
需求，我们选择从⼀家客⼾处回租了部分⾃有设备。如前所述，这在短期内对⽑利率造成了下⾏
压⼒，但也使我们能够在客⼾有需求时及时服务，从⽽拓展现有客⼾、赢得重要新客⼾。
如今，我们已赢得 OpenAI 等前沿实验室，以及 AWS 等超⼤规模云服务商，近期⼜新增了两家超
⼤规模云服务商客⼾。 Cognition 、 Figma 、 Block 、 Built 等领先客⼾也已加⼊我们的云平台。我
们以客⼾需求为导向，因地制宜 —— 有时销售硬件，有时提供云服务。
此前，我们也已说明， 2026 年将是⼀个建设之年 —— 扩⼤算⼒、提升能⼒、积累客⼾，为 2027
年营收实现三倍以上增⻓奠定基础。凭借今年迄今取得的强劲进展，我们有充分信⼼实现这⼀⽬
标。
第三季度业绩
现在转⼊第三季度业绩，我很⾼兴地宣布，第四季度将是我们增⻓和核⼼财务趋势的重要拐点。

我们已连续第三个季度超越核⼼财务指引。我们还提前实现了⾸次⾮ GAAP 净利润转正，早于预
期。
提⽰⼀下，核⼼指标的定义及其与 GAAP 的调节表已包含在今⽇财报发布中，并可在我们的⽹站
上查阅。
核⼼营收：  创历史新⾼，达 2.31 亿美元，环⽐增⻓ 10% ，同⽐增⻓ 70% ，超出我们 2.15 亿美元
指引中点 1600 万美元，超出幅度为 7% 。
核⼼云服务及其他服务营收：  同样创下历史新⾼，同⽐增⻓ 272% ，达 1.45 亿美元。第三季度云
服务营收环⽐增⻓ 13% 。我们⽬前预计第四季度云服务营收将基本持平。这⼀趋势并⾮由定价或
需求问题驱动 —— 定价环境依然健康，我们解决⽅案的需求持续远超供给。
云服务营收的增⻓瓶颈完全在于数据中⼼产能。我们将于 12 ⽉开始提升数据中⼼产能，为明年
积蓄动能，但遗憾的是时间太晚，⽆法对第四季度产⽣实质影响。我们预计 2027 年数据中⼼产
能将出现更为可观的增加，主要增量将从明年年中开始释放，届时我们将交付⽬前已在合同中锁

定的 700 兆⽡数据中⼼产能的较⼤部分。
核⼼硬件营收：  本季度为 8600 万美元，环⽐增⻓ 5% 。我们看到客⼾对快速推理服务的需求不断
增⻓，不仅来⾃我们的私有云，还来⾃与多家新客⼾签署的硬件采购合同。随着这些硬件发货计
划于第四季度启动，核⼼总营收将出现⼤幅跃升。
第三季度⾄第四季度的核⼼营收环⽐增⻓将接近 40% ，同⽐增⻓约为 85% 。这些硬件订单（包括
甲⻣⽂订单）将⾄少持续发货⾄ 2027 年上半年。
正如 Andrew 前⾯提到的，我们今天还宣布微软成为新客⼾。过去⼏个⽉，我们的销售管线规模
增⻓超过 10 倍，驱动因素涵盖多元化机遇，包括超⼤规模云服务商、新兴云服务商、主权云、
企业客⼾和 AI 原⽣公司等各类新⽼客⼾的需求。
⽑利率
第三季度核⼼⽑利率为 41% ，较第⼆季度环⽐改善 40 个基点，并⽐我们的季度指引⾼出 200 个基
点。

### Page 10

除了回租系统对⽑利率的影响外，本季度我们还⾯临⼀些逆⻛因素 —— 来⾃多年前签署的硬件采
购协议（采⽤较低定价）的最后⼀批发货正在消化。我们之前已指引，所有这些因素将造成暂时
性⽑利率下滑，低点将出现在第三季度。实际上，我们的表现优于预期 —— 低点已于上季度（第
⼆季度）出现。
现将第三季度核⼼⽑利率分拆如下：
核⼼硬件⽑利率  环⽐下降⾄ 31% ，主要由年前客⼾协议项下最后批次发货所致。我们⽬前正以
⼤幅提升后的现⾏价格执⾏硬件订单，这也意味着从第四季度起⽑利率将显著改善。
核⼼云服务及其他服务⽑利率  ⼤幅提升⾄ 46.9% ，较第⼆季度提升 510 个基点。主要驱动因素是
以更⾼定价和利润率承接的新业务。部分被从客⼾处回租数据中⼼产能和硬件的较⾼成本所抵
消。我们预计这⼀情况将⾄少持续⾄ 2027 年年中，直⾄我们拥有⾜够的⾃有数据中⼼产能来满
⾜较⾼⽔平的云需求。
综合以上情况，第四季度核⼼⽑利率预计将实现阶梯式改善，提升 700 个基点，⾄中点值 48% 。
我们预计核⼼总⽑利率将在近期超过 50% ，并持续向 60% 的⻓期⽬标推进。

盈利能⼒进展
第三季度及年初⾄今，我们在盈利能⼒⽅⾯取得了强劲进展。核⼼经营利润率环⽐改善 170 个基
点，亏损率降⾄ 14.3% ，超出指引中点近 10 个百分点。核⼼经营亏损与第⼆季度持平，约为
3300 万美元。核⼼净利润转正，达 3000 万美元。年初⾄今调整后核⼼ EBITDA 也实现正值，为
200 万美元。
随着第四季度营收和⽑利率⼤幅增⻓，我们预计所有核⼼关键盈利指标（包括经营利润率）将于
下季度转为正值。
剩余履约义务（ RPO ）与 OpenAI 合同
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，剩余履约义务增⾄ 261 亿美元。我们按季度更新 RPO ，纳⼊新签业务，并根据剩
余承诺期内预计待确认⾦额的变化对现有合同价值进⾏调整。第三季度 RPO 不含 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇之后签
署的新合同影响，包括甲⻣⽂这⼀⼤额合同。
关于 OpenAI 合同的状态，投资者提问颇多，因为其占我们迄今已报告 RPO 的⼤部分。提⽰⼀
下，该合同已在 IPO 前随 S1 ⽂件提交，是⼀份 750 兆⽡算⼒的照付不议（ take-or-pay ）合同。

合同包含众多对双⽅均适⽤的条款，其中有⼀条规定：若我们在交付算⼒⽅⾯出现延误，
OpenAI 有权在此期间获得抵扣额度，⽤于未来的承诺采购。我们已计算潜在延误抵扣额度，并
已在 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的 RPO 中予以扣除。
这些抵扣对营收和⽑利率的影响较⼩，原因有⼆：⼀是占 RPO 的⽐例相对较⼩；⼆是绝⼤部分
营收将在未来数年内按⽐例确认，抵扣⾦额将随之分摊。未来每个季度将根据最新估算进⾏更
新，并在必要时反映在指引中。
若算⼒交付延误持续达 90 天或以上， OpenAI 和 Cerebras 双⽅均有权取消该部分算⼒承诺（仅限
该部分）。我们估计今年可能⾯临取消⻛险的算⼒量，将低于总承诺兆⽡数的 5% 。
鉴于全球数据中⼼产能需求旺盛，我们在寻找并上线初始数据中⼼产能以满⾜交付计划⽅⾯，相
较极为紧张的时间表花费了额外时间。根据我们团队对各站点的频繁实地检查，我们⽬前预计将
于 2027 年下半年追回进度，届时未来延误抵扣⻛险应会相应降低。

### Page 11

我们采取了保守态度，未假设任何延误将因超出我们控制范围的外部依赖因素⽽被豁免。若豁免
情况发⽣，将减少我们所预估的延误抵扣⾦额。
此外，即便 OpenAI 或 Cerebras 选择取消某批次延误算⼒，我们认为凭借充⾜的市场需求，完全
可以找到其他客⼾承接。
OpenAI 还保留了在初始 750 兆⽡承诺之外，另⾏采购最多 1.25 吉⽡算⼒的选择权，最早可于
2029 年起上线。鉴于这还有三年时间，且 AI 领域变化之快，我们尚未就此展开讨论。
资产负债表与资本效率
我们继续保持强劲的流动性。第三季度末，现⾦、现⾦等价物、受限现⾦及有价证券合计约 78
亿美元。我们还拥有最⾼ 8.5 亿美元的循环信贷额度，⽬前尚未动⽤。
今年 1 ⽉获得的 10 亿美元预付流动资⾦贷款，以及作为 OpenAI 合同部分内容⽤于新数据中⼼产
能装修的⾮经常性直通费⽤的持续收款，已为今年迄今⼤部分净营运资本需求和资本⽀出提供了
资⾦。
我们不是⼀家新兴云服务商，⽽是业界最快推理解决⽅案的垂直整合发明者。我们的业务具备技

术差异化所赋予的韧性，我们相信这将在⻓期内驱动更⾼的利润潜⼒和现⾦创造能⼒。
我们的模式在已部署算⼒设备的资本投⼊⽅⾯也较低。对于我们在数据中⼼⾃主制造的⼤量设备
—— 与其他选择⾃研并部署 AI 芯⽚的公司类似 —— 我们⽆需向第三⽅⽀付 70% ⽑利率，也⽆需使
⽤⾼价 HBM ，只需⽀付我们的物料清单（ BOM ）成本。这既降低了每兆⽡的相对资本⽀出，⼜
使我们能够⽣产最快速、最有价值的 token 。
这反映在我们的资本结构中 —— 尽管我们私有云建设规模快速扩张，资产负债表上唯⼀的债务仅
为 OpenAI 流动资⾦贷款。我们还持续从客⼾处获得预付款，这是提升资本效率的另⼀杠杆。
我们当前的资本结构包含充裕的现⾦和投资，负债极少，为我们以多种⽅式持续融资、⽀撑快速
增⻓提供了充分灵活性 —— 这在当前资本市场环境下尤为有利。
展望

总结

### Page 12

第三季度是 Cerebras ⼜⼀创纪录的季度，第四季度亦将如此。我们灵活的硬件与云服务兼售模
式正在推动营收强劲增⻓，⽑利率改善也⽐预期提前了整整⼀个季度。
我们在制造和数据中⼼产能规模化⽅⾯取得了显著进展，这将使我们能够在 2027 年实现营收三
倍增⻓，同时核⼼⽑利率向 60% 的⻓期⽬标持续推进。我们已展现出强劲的运营杠杆效应，并预
计第四季度核⼼经营利润将基本实现盈亏平衡，此后预计在 2027 年⼤幅向 40% 的⻓期经营利润
率⽬标迈进。
这种规模、增⻓与盈利能⼒的综合优势，使我们成为独具特⾊的领先 AI 系统提供商。
最后补充⼀点：提醒⼤家，我们上市已逾 180 天， IPO 股票锁定期已于 11 ⽉ 9 ⽇全部届满解锁。

### Page 13

Andrew Feldman ：
这是个⾮常好的问题。 Profit 公司在其 S1 或某条推⽂中提到，他们只⽤了约三天时间完成了 AMD
GPU 的迁移上线。
这是⼀个⾮常有意义且重要的进展，尤其对于⻓期视⻆的观察者⽽⾔。我认为，业界尚未充分认
识到这对我们⽣态系统意味着什么。
编译器和底层软件历来是新架构⾯临的根本障碍，它们天然有利于在位者，也是⼈们常说的 " 软
件护城河 " 的核⼼来源。如果 Profit 公司真能实现他们所说的，那就意味着底层软件的价值将收
缩，并随时间趋近于消失。软件护城河也将随之收窄并消弭。
若底层软件的价值趋于零，真正能够提供价值的，就只剩下创新硬件。这意味着竞争分化将更多
取决于硬件的差异化程度。
我认为，这对 Cerebras 这样的创新硬件公司 —— 以及其他采⽤全新架构的硬件先⾏者 —— 是巨
⼤的优势，同时将削弱在位者⻓期以来享有的优势地位。
这个问题问得很好，值得整个⾏业密切关注。谢谢。

Quinn Bolton （ Needham ）：
⼤家好，祝贺取得进展。 Andrew ，我想确认⼀下我理解是否正确：在 RPO 和您的假设中，您已

经就潜在延误抵扣做了预提，但⽬前尚未收到任何关于延误算⼒的取消通知，是这样吗？
Andrew Feldman：正确。
Quinn Bolton：
好的，很好。第⼆个问题：市场上有⼀种担忧，认为低批次 GPU 能够实现快速推理。听起来那
是⼀种成本很⾼的 token 交付⽅式。能否⽐较⼀下 Cerebras 与低批次 GPU 配置的每 token 成本？
Andrew Feldman ：

### Page 15

当然。 GPU 可以通过降低批次规模来换取更⾼的速度，但代价是牺牲吞吐量 —— 低批次下吞吐
量很低，⽽当你试图提⾼速度时， GPU 会进⼊低批次区间，总吞吐量变得⾮常⼩。
这是由架构特性决定的。可以这样理解： GPU 通过降批次确实能获得⼀定程度的性能提升，但
这样做时，每 token 成本会急剧上升 —— 同时服务的⽤⼾数⼤幅减少，总 token ⽣产量极为有限。
随着速度要求进⼀步提⾼，我们的优势会越来越强。当速度达到 GPU ⽆论如何都⽆法提供的⽔
平时， Cerebras 是唯⼀的选项 —— 在批次为 1 的情况下， GPU 仍然⽆法与我们媲美。
关于成本交叉点：⼤约在每秒 250 ⾄ 300 个 token 时，我们在经济性上更有优势；到每秒 500 、
800 、 1000 个 token 时，我们的优势会远超对⼿；在 800 ⾄ 2000 个 token 的区间， GPU 根本⽆法参
与竞争。这就是当前竞争格局的基本⾯貌。这个问题涉及复杂话题，提得很好。
Quinn Bolton：谢谢， Andrew 。

接线员：感谢提问。下⼀个问题来⾃ TD Cowen 的 Joshua Bruckheimer 。请提问。
Joshua Bruckheimer （ TD Cowen ）：
⼤家好，感谢接受提问，并祝贺微软合作顺利落地。⾸先，请谈谈在⼟地、电⼒和建筑问题的背
景下，您是如何分配算⼒的？看起来硅⽚和机架供应不是问题，那么在增量硬件销售⽅⾯还有多
⼤ 空 间 ？ 另 外 ， 鉴 于 云 客 ⼾ ⾯ 临 ⼟ 地 、 电 ⼒ 和 建 筑 ⽅ ⾯ 的 挑 战 ， 是 否 与 OpenAI 讨 论 过 由
OpenAI 或其合作伙伴购买硬件并部署在他们⾃⼰的数据中⼼？谢谢。
Andrew Feldman：这个问题确实有两层。 Josh ，第⼀层是：⼟地、电⼒和建筑及其施⼯是否
真的是限制因素？答案是肯定的。台积电已向我们做出了 2027 年充⾜晶圆产能的承诺，我们对
此⾮常有把握。不使⽤ HBM 显然也是极⼤的优势，因为 HBM ⽬前供应极为紧张。
你第⼆层问题问得很好：这是否为我们在数据中⼼建设期间销售硬件创造了机会？唯⼀需要注意

的是，据我们所知，⼏乎所有⼈的数据中⼼建设都在延期 —— 包括 OpenAI ，包括我们知道的所
有超⼤规模云服务商数据中⼼。数据中⼼延期是全球性的普遍现象，并⾮我们独有，我们的客⼾
同样⾯临这⼀制约。所以，答案是：是的，我们有产品供应；是的，我们正在向拥有数据中⼼的
客⼾发货；但同样，当前数据中⼼普遍延期，确实影响了实际进展。是否清楚了？
Joshua Bruckheimer ：
明⽩，但三问中的最后⼀个，也是我提的问题⾥⽐较难听的⼀部分 ——OpenAI 合同是否有可能
转为硬件采购？
Andrew Feldman ：
抱歉，合同中确实赋予了他们这⼀权利，但据我们了解，他们⾃⼰的数据中⼼建设同样落后于计
划。
Joshua Bruckheimer ：好的，明⽩了。谢谢。那么跟进问题 ——
Andrew Feldman ：Josh ，这已经是第七问了。
Joshua Bruckheimer：确实我的问题，我承认。能否谈谈甲⻣⽂和微软是如何向他们的客⼾定
位 Cerebras 的产品和服务？这是否主要体现为硬件销售，然后根据超快推理需求来驱动客⼾使

⽤量？谢谢。

## Document 304: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

## Document 307: CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline

Source PDF: all report pdf/CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 8

### Page 1

The Bank Board increased interest rates to 4% in a close call,
and we see valid arguments for both positions. Second-round
eﬀects will push inﬂation above the upper bound of the target
corridor for some time, while dwindling economic activity
warrants lots of caution. We see neither hikes nor cuts as the
most likely outcome for quite some time
David Havrlant
The Czech National Bank's decision to raise rates on 5 November was a close
call
Assessing the impact of Hormuz

The Czech National Bank increased the policy rate by 25bp to 4% in
a split vote on 5 November, with Governor Ales Michl and board
members Jan Kubicek, Jan Prochazka and Jakub Seidler voting for
a hike. Meanwhile, Deputy Governors Eva Zamrazilova and Jan Frait,
along with board member Karina Kubelkova, favoured interest rate
stability. We assess the current situation and the risks to the
outlook as complex and see relevant justiﬁcation for both options,

depending on whether your concerns are more on the side of

### Page 2

second-round inﬂationary eﬀects or on the side of disappointing
economic activity and the consequences for consumption.
The Bank Board assessed overall risks as pro-inﬂationary. There
was an agreement that the secondary impacts of the energy shock
have not been signiﬁcantly reﬂected in domestic inﬂation so far.
However, as the Hormuz conﬂict becomes more protracted, the
likelihood of tangible second-round eﬀects increases. Jakub Seidler
noted that global energy prices have been elevated for too long, so

it would be diﬃcult to avoid a pass-through to other segments of
consumer prices. Deputy Governor Zamrazilova emphasised that
much depends on further developments in the Middle Eastern
conﬂict. Should tensions ease in the near future, a rate hike at the
November meeting would not be necessary. The key question is
whether the conﬂict will eventually pass or become the new
normal, with the latter implying persistently high energy costs.
Deputy Governor Frait stated that energy prices remaining at these

levels for such an extended period could have a negative impact on
demand and economic activity, mainly by reducing the real
incomes of households and companies. The current negative
supply shock has hit the Czech economy from a favourable starting
position, but the factors that helped keep inﬂation close to the
target this year are about to fade. The increase in longer-term rates
contributes to the restrictive stance of monetary policy, and implies

some softening in domestic lending activity. The board members
agreed that this is a key factor reducing the need for further
tightening for the time being.
To rise or not to rise?
However, as Jan Kubicek noted, core inﬂation is not slowing down
as previously assumed and is even slightly increasing. This
represents a fundamental concern, especially in combination with
the disappointing real growth performance. That said, producer
prices have embarked on an upward trend and may put further

pressure on the core rate. Jakub Seidler and Karina Kubelkova
emphasised that the renewed price increases in the energy and
food segment are usually the key drivers when it comes to inﬂation

### Page 3

expectations, and the ﬁrst signs of an increase are already visible.
And we all know that unanchored inﬂation expectations are one of
the things that keep central bankers awake at night.
Producer prices will drive consumer prices
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
En gros, among the main reasons for a rate hike in November were:
i) persistent core inﬂation, ii) still robust wage dynamics and iii)
second-round eﬀects expected to bite soon. Among the main

reasons for staying on hold and evaluating were: i) secondary
inﬂationary impacts are so far limited, ii) long-term rates deliver
restriction, iii) disappointing economic activity.
We see the arguments in both directions as valid, which made the
November decision relatively complex and was reﬂected in the
close vote. Time will tell whether the second-round eﬀects that we
believe are just kicking in will prove signiﬁcant without weighing too
heavily on economic activity. Or whether the deteriorating real

purchasing power and dwindling real wage growth could lead to
weaker household consumption, which would in turn put
downward pressure on inﬂation. Both eﬀects are certainly
interconnected, which only underscores the beauty of
macroeconomics, and the formidable power of prices to clear the
market.

### Page 4

Punchy inﬂation across price segments
When it comes to our inﬂation forecast, we deﬁnitely see pro-
inﬂationary pressures when looking ahead. We expect annual
producer price growth to peak at around 7% in industry early next
year and at around 12% in agriculture mid-next year. At the same
time, the annual pace of import prices already quickened to above
8% in September. Those are not low growth rates, and all are going
to feed into consumer prices sooner or later. At the same time, the

koruna continues to weaken against the dollar, making imports
pricier. With all that on board, we see the yearly average of inﬂation
picking up from 2.1% this year to 3.2% next, reaching a peak of just
below 4% in February.
Inﬂation to hover above the target for
longer
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond

distributors and factor in a 3.5% increase in electricity prices, a

6.5% increase in natural gas prices, an 8% increase in heating
prices and a 6% rise in water charges. Nonetheless, not all
distributors will apply the same increase and not all households will
be aﬀected equally. Based on our best estimate, we expect a 3%
monthly increase in regulated prices in January, resulting in a 3.8%

year-on-year gain. However, should global oil and European natural

Regulated and food prices turn pro-

Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
Food prices are set to shift from an anti-inﬂationary position this

year to an inﬂationary one, averaging just below 3% next year, but
peaking at around 4%. That said, we sense quite some upward risks
here, especially should energy prices stay elevated for longer, which
would propel fertiliser prices and transport costs.
Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline
When it comes to core inﬂation, two opposing forces are set for a
duel: second-round eﬀects, coupled with rising import prices,
against a softening housing market and crumbling real purchasing

power. Timing is of the essence. We expect the former to strike ﬁrst,
but the latter to respond without much hesitation. For now, we see
the forces as roughly balanced, keeping average core inﬂation just
below 3% next year.

Consumption could slow tangibly along with
real wages
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
So yes, upbeat inﬂation across price segments warrants restrictive
monetary policy. Meanwhile, weakening economic activity
warrants a lot of caution when it comes to any future rate hikes.
The thing is, we see real GDP growth averaging just 1.7% this year
and lifting oﬀ only gradually to 2.1% next year. Meanwhile, we see
household consumption slowing tangibly to 1.8% next year from

2.5% this year. Indeed, we are likely positioned at the lower end of
the forecast range, yet we take the view that a slowdown in real
wage growth will eventually take its toll. Therefore, we see neither
further hikes nor cuts when looking way ahead, with the 4% policy
rate settling in as the new normal.

bond yields. In contrast, should we slip into an episode of stagnant
growth in Europe for whatever reason, our hypothesis of a 4%

nominal interest rate becoming the new normal would start to look
a little shakier, at least temporarily.

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026
